## 4) 代码（直接复制）
### filter_engine.py（AC 自动机 + normalize + 命中位置）
```python
# filter_engine.py
import re
import unicodedata
import ahocorasick
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict

ZERO_WIDTH = dict.fromkeys(map(ord, ["\u200b", "\u200c", "\u200d", "\ufeff"]), None)

SEP_PATTERN = re.compile(r"[\s\*\·\•\.\-\_\~\|\/\\、，。；;:：\(\)\[\]\{\}<>《》“”\"'`]+", re.UNICODE)

@dataclass
class Hit:
    word: str
    level: str  # "red" or "gray"
    start: int
    end: int

def normalize_text(s: str) -> str:
    if not s:
        return ""
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s)
    s = s.translate(ZERO_WIDTH)
    s = s.lower()
    s = SEP_PATTERN.sub("", s)
    return s

class ACAutomaton:
    def __init__(self):
        self.A = ahocorasick.Automaton()

    def add_words(self, words: List[Tuple[str, str]]):
        # words: [(word, level), ...]
        for w, lvl in words:
            nw = normalize_text(w.strip())
            if not nw:
                continue
            # store original word (for display) and level
            self.A.add_word(nw, (w.strip(), lvl))
        self.A.make_automaton()

    def find(self, text: str) -> List[Hit]:
        nt = normalize_text(text)
        hits: List[Hit] = []
        for end_idx, (orig_word, lvl) in self.A.iter(nt):
            start_idx = end_idx - len(normalize_text(orig_word)) + 1
            hits.append(Hit(word=orig_word, level=lvl, start=start_idx, end=end_idx + 1))
        # 去重/合并重叠：保留更长的命中
        hits.sort(key=lambda h: (h.start, -(h.end - h.start)))
        merged: List[Hit] = []
        last_end = -1
        for h in hits:
            if h.start >= last_end:
                merged.append(h)
                last_end = h.end
        return merged

def load_wordlists(red_path="words_red.txt", gray_path="words_gray.txt", whitelist_path="whitelist.txt"):
    def read_lines(p):
        try:
            with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                return [x.strip() for x in f.readlines() if x.strip() and not x.strip().startswith("#")]
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return []

    red = read_lines(red_path)
    gray = read_lines(gray_path)
    white = read_lines(whitelist_path)
    return red, gray, white

def apply_whitelist(text: str, whitelist: List[str]) -> str:
    # 直接把白名单短语替换成空，避免误杀（粗暴但好用）
    out = text
    for w in whitelist:
        if w:
            out = out.replace(w, "")
    return out
```

### report.py（HTML 报告：目录 + 高亮 + 命中表 + 上下文）
```python
# report.py
import html
from typing import List, Dict
from filter_engine import Hit

def _escape(s: str) -> str:
    return html.escape(s or "", quote=True)

def build_context_snippets(raw_text: str, hits: List[Hit], window: int = 24) -> List[Dict]:
    # 注意：Hit 的 start/end 是基于 normalize 后的位置
    # MVP：上下文用“原文里直接查找词”来定位（更贴近你人工审）
    snippets = []
    lower = raw_text.lower()
    for h in hits:
        w = h.word.lower()
        idx = lower.find(w)
        if idx == -1:
            continue
        s = max(0, idx - window)
        e = min(len(raw_text), idx + len(h.word) + window)
        snippets.append({
            "word": h.word,
            "level": h.level,
            "start": idx,
            "end": idx + len(h.word),
            "snippet": raw_text[s:e],
        })
    return snippets

def highlight_html(raw_text: str, words_red: List[str], words_gray: List[str]) -> str:
    # 先转义，再用简单替换高亮（MVP）
    safe = _escape(raw_text)
    # 为避免互相覆盖，先灰后红
    for w in sorted(set(words_gray), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w: 
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(_escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#fff3bf;">{_escape(w)}</mark>')
    for w in sorted(set(words_red), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w:
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(_escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#ffd6d6;">{_escape(w)}</mark>')
    return safe

def build_html_report(code: str, title: str, text: str, hits: List[Hit], words_red: List[str], words_gray: List[str]) -> str:
    red_hits = [h for h in hits if h.level == "red"]
    gray_hits = [h for h in hits if h.level == "gray"]

    decision = "PASS"
    if red_hits:
        decision = "BLOCK"
    elif gray_hits:
        decision = "REVIEW"

    snippets = build_context_snippets(text, hits)

    # 命中表
    rows = []
    for i, h in enumerate(hits, 1):
        rows.append(f"<tr><td>{i}</td><td>{_escape(h.level)}</td><td>{_escape(h.word)}</td></tr>")
    hit_table = "\n".join(rows) if rows else "<tr><td colspan='3'>无命中</td></tr>"

    # 上下文
    sn_html = []
    for i, s in enumerate(snippets, 1):
        sn_html.append(
            f"<div style='margin:8px 0;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:8px;'>"
            f"<div><b>{i}. [{_escape(s['level'])}] {_escape(s['word'])}</b></div>"
            f"<div style='white-space:pre-wrap;font-family:ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;'>"
            f"{_escape(s['snippet'])}"
            f"</div></div>"
        )
    snippets_block = "\n".join(sn_html) if sn_html else "<div>无上下文片段</div>"

    body_highlight = highlight_html(text, words_red, words_gray)

    html_doc = f"""<!doctype html>
<html lang="zh">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/>
<title>Review {code}</title>
</head>
<body style="font-family:ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Helvetica, Arial; padding:24px; line-height:1.55;">
  <h2 style="margin:0 0 8px 0;">审核报告</h2>
  <div style="color:#666;margin-bottom:16px;">
    <div><b>code</b>: {_escape(code)}</div>
    <div><b>标题</b>: {_escape(title)}</div>
    <div><b>判定</b>: <span style="padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid #ddd;">{decision}</span></div>
  </div>

  <div style="margin:16px 0; padding:12px; border:1px solid #eee; border-radius:12px;">
    <b>目录</b>
    <ol>
      <li><a href="#hits">命中总览</a></li>
      <li><a href="#snippets">命中上下文</a></li>
      <li><a href="#full">全文（高亮）</a></li>
    </ol>
  </div>

  <h3 id="hits">命中总览</h3>
  <table style="border-collapse:collapse; width:100%; margin:8px 0;">
    <thead>
      <tr>
        <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">#</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">level</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">word</th>
      </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody>
      {hit_table}
    </tbody>
  </table>

  <h3 id="snippets">命中上下文</h3>
  {snippets_block}

  <h3 id="full">全文（高亮）</h3>
  <div style="white-space:pre-wrap; border:1px solid #eee; border-radius:12px; padding:12px;">
    {body_highlight}
  </div>

  <div style="margin-top:24px;color:#999;font-size:12px;">
    tip: 直接在邮箱里搜 <b>{_escape(code)}</b> 回溯
  </div>
</body>
</html>
"""
    return html_doc, decision
```

### send_gmail.py（发 HTML 正文）
```python
# send_gmail.py
import os
import smtplib
from email.message import EmailMessage

def send_gmail_html(subject: str, html_body: str, sender: str, app_password: str, to_addr: str):
    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = sender
    msg["To"] = to_addr
    msg.set_content("你的邮箱客户端不支持 HTML，请用网页版或支持 HTML 的客户端查看。")
    msg.add_alternative(html_body, subtype="html")

    with smtplib.SMTP("smtp.gmail.com", 587) as s:
        s.ehlo()
        s.starttls()
        s.login(sender, app_password)
        s.send_message(msg)
```

### send_tg.py（可选：发封面图到 Telegram）
```python
# send_tg.py
import requests

def send_telegram_photo(bot_token: str, chat_id: str, image_path: str, caption: str):
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{bot_token}/sendPhoto"
    with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
        files = {"photo": f}
        data = {"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": caption}
        r = requests.post(url, data=data, files=files, timeout=60)
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()
```

### run_job.py（主入口：读文本 -> 扫词 -> 生成报告 -> 发 Gmail；可选发 Telegram 图；最后删除本地临时文件）
```python
# run_job.py
import os
import argparse
from dotenv import load_dotenv

from filter_engine import ACAutomaton, load_wordlists, apply_whitelist
from report import build_html_report
from send_gmail import send_gmail_html

def read_text(path: str) -> str:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()

def main():
    load_dotenv()

    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--code", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--title", default="")
    p.add_argument("--text", required=True, help="path to text file (subtitle/title/desc merged)")
    p.add_argument("--thumb", default="", help="optional thumbnail image path to forward to Telegram")
    p.add_argument("--delete_thumb", action="store_true", help="delete thumb file after sending")
    args = p.parse_args()

    red, gray, white = load_wordlists()
    text_raw = read_text(args.text)
    text_raw = apply_whitelist(text_raw, white)

    ac = ACAutomaton()
    ac.add_words([(w, "red") for w in red] + [(w, "gray") for w in gray])
    hits = ac.find(text_raw)

    html_doc, decision = build_html_report(
        code=args.code,
        title=args.title,
        text=text_raw,
        hits=hits,
        words_red=red,
        words_gray=gray,
    )

    sender = os.environ["GMAIL_SENDER"]
    app_pw = os.environ["GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD"]
    to_addr = os.environ["GMAIL_TO"]

    subject = f"[{decision}] code={args.code} | {args.title[:60]}"
    send_gmail_html(subject, html_doc, sender, app_pw, to_addr)

    # Optional: forward thumbnail to Telegram (image-only, no OCR needed)
    if args.thumb:
        bot = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "")
        chat_id = os.getenv("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "")
        if bot and chat_id:
            from send_tg import send_telegram_photo
            caption = f"code={args.code}\n{args.title[:80]}"
            send_telegram_photo(bot, chat_id, args.thumb, caption)

        if args.delete_thumb:
            try:
                os.remove(args.thumb)
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

---

## 5) 使用方式
### A) 单条审核（最常用）
1) 把你要审核的文本合成一个文件，比如 `AB12-3CD4.txt`
2) 运行：
```bash
python run_job.py --code AB12-3CD4 --title "xxx视频标题" --text AB12-3CD4.txt
```

### B) 带封面图发 Telegram（可选）
```bash
python run_job.py --code AB12-3CD4 --title "xxx视频标题" --text AB12-3CD4.txt --thumb AB12-3CD4.jpg --delete_thumb
```

### C) 定时
用 cron/systemd timer 都行，核心就是批量循环跑 `run_job.py`。

---

## 你现在最关键的“舒服点”
- **不再发 txt 附件**：邮件正文就是可读的 HTML（目录 + 高亮）  
- Telegram 只做“翻封面图”，不承载长文本  
- `code` 是唯一主键：两边搜同一个 `code` 就能回溯

---

如果你下一步要把“字幕 SRT 带时间段”也做进邮件目录里（点击目录直接跳到 00:01:23 那段），我可以在 `report.py` 里把 **SRT 解析 + 时间段锚点**加进去，仍然保持轻量。

### 我
<!-- mid: f59718ec-852b-4e47-8cee-2ec9f7de0cc8 -->
发邮件，发电报，是否花费钱，

### AI
<!-- mid: 05faabe3-ed40-444f-837c-c15902df438c -->
**基本不花钱。**你这套“Gmail + Telegram”在个人用量下几乎都是 **¥0**，成本主要是你自己的网络和服务器电费/VPS（如果你跑在服务器上）。

## 1) 发 Gmail 邮件要不要钱？
- ✅ **用 Gmail SMTP + App Password：不收费**
- ❗限制：有**发送额度/频率**（防滥用）。正常每天几十封、几百封以内通常没事；如果你突然群发上千封，可能触发限制或临时封禁。

**隐性成本**：无。只要你不是走第三方邮件服务（SendGrid/Mailgun 之类，那才可能收费）。

## 2) 发 Telegram 要不要钱？
- ✅ **Telegram Bot 发消息/图片：不收费**
- ❗限制：有**速率限制**（防刷屏），但你每天发几十张封面图完全不构成问题。

## 3) 真正可能花钱的是哪里？
- **服务器/VPS**：如果你把脚本放云服务器跑，那是 VPS 费用（你也可以本机跑，¥0）。
- **短信/电话类通知**：你没用到，忽略。
- **第三方 OCR API**：你如果改成用云 OCR 才会收费；你现在用本地 Tesseract 就是 ¥0。

---

### 最简结论
- **Gmail 发邮件：¥0（个人正常用量）**
- **Telegram 发图/消息：¥0**
- **可能的成本只有：你用的服务器（可选）**

如果你告诉我你大概每天要发多少封邮件、多少张图，我可以按这个量级给你一个“不会触发限制”的节流策略（比如合并成每日汇总邮件）。

如果你用的是**普通 Gmail 个人号**，那 **500 并不是“小于上限”，而是“正好顶到上限”**，很容易触发限流。citeturn1search0

### Gmail 发邮件是否收费
- **¥0**（SMTP + App Password 也不收费）
- 但有配额：
  - **个人 Gmail：>500 封/滚动 24 小时** 会报“达到发送限制”，通常 **1–24 小时**才能恢复。citeturn1search0
  - **Google Workspace 付费号：2,000 封/天**；试用期可能只有 500。citeturn0search0

### 所以你问“500 小于…？”
- **个人 Gmail：500 ≈ 天花板，不建议跑满。**citeturn1search0  
- 你如果每天真有 500 个 `code` 要回溯，正确姿势是：**别一条一封**，改成：
  1) **每日/每小时汇总邮件**（一封邮件里包含 N 个 code 的目录+高亮）  
  2) 或者 **每 50 个 code 合并一封**（10 封/天，比 500 封安全太多）

### Telegram 发图是否收费
- **¥0**（Bot 发消息/图片免费）

- Telegram：照常当“封面相册”。

## 规则定死（你要的“一次 10 个 code”）
- **队列**：每个待审核任务落一个 `code`（文本/字幕等）
- **触发**：队列里 **>=10** 个就发一封邮件
- **邮件内容**：一封邮件里放 10 个 code 的目录 + 每个 code 的高亮全文 + 命中表
- **封面图**：仍然可选发到 entity["company","Telegram","messaging app"]（每个 code 一张，caption 带 code），邮件里不塞图

> 这样你回溯只要搜 `code`：  
> 邮箱搜到那封“10 合 1”的邮件；Telegram 搜到那张封面图。

---

## 邮件格式（推荐模板）
**Subject**
```
[REVIEW BATCH] 10 codes | AB12-3CD4 … Q9N1-7K3P
```

**HTML 正文目录**
- 10 个 code 的列表（可点跳转）
- 每个 code 一个 section：
  - 标题（可选）
  - 判定（PASS/REVIEW/BLOCK）
  - 命中词表
  - 命中上下文
  - 全文高亮

---

## 最小实现（在你现有脚本上加一个 batch 入口）
你只需要新增一个 `batch_run.py`，从 `queue/` 里取 10 个任务拼成一封发出去。

### 约定一个队列目录
```
queue/
  AB12-3CD4/
    title.txt
    text.txt
  K2P0-88JD/
    title.txt
    text.txt
...
sent/
```

### batch_run.py（核心逻辑）
```python
import os, shutil
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from filter_engine import ACAutomaton, load_wordlists, apply_whitelist
from report import build_html_report
from send_gmail import send_gmail_html

QUEUE_DIR = "queue"
SENT_DIR = "sent"
BATCH_SIZE = 10

def read_file(p, default=""):
    try:
        with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return f.read().strip()
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return default

def list_codes():
    if not os.path.isdir(QUEUE_DIR):
        return []
    codes = [d for d in os.listdir(QUEUE_DIR) if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(QUEUE_DIR, d))]
    codes.sort()
    return codes

def wrap_batch_html(items):
    # items: [(code, title, decision, section_html), ...]
    toc = "\n".join([f"<li><a href='#{c}'>{c}</a> - {d} - {title}</li>"
                     for c, title, d, _ in items])
    sections = "\n".join([f"<hr/><h2 id='{c}'>code={c} <span style='font-size:12px;color:#666'>[{d}]</span></h2>"
                          f"<div style='color:#666;margin-bottom:8px;'><b>标题</b>: {title}</div>"
                          f"{sec}"
                          for c, title, d, sec in items])

    return f"""<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/></head>
<body style="font-family:system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Helvetica, Arial; padding:24px;">
  <h1 style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">审核批次报告（10 合 1）</h1>
  <div style="margin:12px 0; padding:12px; border:1px solid #eee; border-radius:12px;">
    <b>目录</b>
    <ol>{toc}</ol>
  </div>
  {sections}
</body></html>
"""

def main():
    load_dotenv()
    os.makedirs(SENT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    codes = list_codes()
    if len(codes) < BATCH_SIZE:
        print(f"Queue < {BATCH_SIZE}, skip.")
        return

    batch = codes[:BATCH_SIZE]

    red, gray, white = load_wordlists()
    ac = ACAutomaton()
    ac.add_words([(w, "red") for w in red] + [(w, "gray") for w in gray])

    items = []
    for code in batch:
        base = os.path.join(QUEUE_DIR, code)
        title = read_file(os.path.join(base, "title.txt"), "")
        text = read_file(os.path.join(base, "text.txt"), "")
        text = apply_whitelist(text, white)
        hits = ac.find(text)

        # build_html_report 目前返回的是“完整HTML文档 + decision”
        # 我们只需要里面的主体部分，所以用一个简单技巧：取 <body> 内部
        full_html, decision = build_html_report(code, title, text, hits, red, gray)
        body = full_html.split("<body", 1)[1]
        body = body.split(">", 1)[1].rsplit("</body>", 1)[0]
        items.append((code, title[:60], decision, body))

    html_batch = wrap_batch_html(items)

    sender = os.environ["GMAIL_SENDER"]
    app_pw  = os.environ["GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD"]
    to_addr = os.environ["GMAIL_TO"]

    subject = f"[REVIEW BATCH] 10 codes | {batch[0]} … {batch[-1]}"
    send_gmail_html(subject, html_batch, sender, app_pw, to_addr)

    # 发送成功后，移动到 sent/
    for code in batch:
        src = os.path.join(QUEUE_DIR, code)
        dst = os.path.join(SENT_DIR, code)
        if os.path.exists(dst):
            shutil.rmtree(dst)
        shutil.move(src, dst)

    print("Sent batch:", batch)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

---

## 你怎么用（最省脑）
1) 每来一个任务，就把它落到 `queue/<code>/text.txt`（标题可选 `title.txt`）  
2) 定时跑：
```bash
python batch_run.py
```
- 队列 >=10：发一封  
- 队列 <10：不发（继续攒）

根据我对项目代码的分析，您当前使用的文字识别技术主要是基于**规则匹配的文本内容审核系统**，而非传统的光学字符识别（OCR）技术。具体来说：

### 核心技术分析

1. **文本预处理**：
   - 使用 `normalize_text` 函数（main.py:179-186）对文本进行标准化处理
   - 包括转小写、移除非字母数字字符、压缩空格等操作

2. **敏感内容检测**：
   - 使用 `contains_banned_names` 函数（main.py:189-194）检测文本中是否包含敏感词汇
   - 基于预定义的 `banned_names` 列表进行匹配

3. **前端审核界面**：
   - 在 4.md 文件中的 `ToolsPage` 组件实现了完整的切片审核系统前端
   - 包含文本输入、审核处理、结果展示等功能

4. **审核逻辑**：
   - 前端模拟了审核过程，使用正则表达式检测敏感内容（4.md:219）
   - 生成审核结果，包括状态、评分、详细项和总结

### 技术特点

- **基于规则的检测**：使用预定义规则和正则表达式进行内容审核
- **前端模拟处理**：核心审核逻辑在前端实现，通过 setTimeout 模拟异步处理
- **无OCR依赖**：项目中没有集成专门的OCR库（如Tesseract、EasyOCR等）
- **用户认证**：集成了Google OAuth认证系统，保护审核功能

### 应用场景

这个系统主要用于**文本内容的合规性审核**，特别适合：
- 短视频切片的文本内容审核
- 社交媒体内容的敏感词过滤
- 平台内容的质量评估

## Step 2：新增一个真正的文本检测模块
新建 `app/text_filter.py`（核心引擎，后端复用）

```python
# app/text_filter.py
import os, re, unicodedata
import ahocorasick
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict

ZERO_WIDTH = dict.fromkeys(map(ord, ["\u200b","\u200c","\u200d","\ufeff"]), None)
SEP_RE = re.compile(r"[\s\*\·\•\.\-\_\~\|\/\\、，。；;:：\(\)\[\]\{\}<>《》“”\"'`]+", re.UNICODE)

def normalize_text(s: str) -> str:
    if not s:
        return ""
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", s)
    s = s.translate(ZERO_WIDTH)
    s = s.lower()
    # 关键：去掉常见干扰符 + 空白
    s = SEP_RE.sub("", s)
    return s

def load_words(path: str) -> List[str]:
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return []
    out = []
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            t = line.strip()
            if not t or t.startswith("#"):
                continue
            out.append(t)
    return out

@dataclass
class Hit:
    word: str
    level: str  # red/gray
    start: int
    end: int

class TextFilter:
    def __init__(self, red_path: str, gray_path: str, whitelist_path: str):
        self.red_path = red_path
        self.gray_path = gray_path
        self.whitelist_path = whitelist_path
        self._build()

    def _build(self):
        self.red = load_words(self.red_path)
        self.gray = load_words(self.gray_path)
        self.white = load_words(self.whitelist_path)

        A = ahocorasick.Automaton()
        for w in self.red:
            nw = normalize_text(w)
            if nw:
                A.add_word(nw, (w, "red"))
        for w in self.gray:
            nw = normalize_text(w)
            if nw:
                A.add_word(nw, (w, "gray"))
        A.make_automaton()
        self.A = A

    def reload(self):
        self._build()

    def apply_whitelist(self, text: str) -> str:
        # MVP：白名单短语直接从原文里抹掉，降低误杀
        out = text or ""
        for w in self.white:
            if w:
                out = out.replace(w, "")
        return out

    def scan(self, text: str) -> Dict:
        raw = self.apply_whitelist(text)
        nt = normalize_text(raw)

        hits: List[Hit] = []
        for end_idx, (orig, lvl) in self.A.iter(nt):
            start_idx = end_idx - len(normalize_text(orig)) + 1
            hits.append(Hit(orig, lvl, start_idx, end_idx + 1))

        # 简单去重：同起点保留最长
        hits.sort(key=lambda h: (h.start, -(h.end - h.start)))
        merged = []
        last_end = -1
        for h in hits:
            if h.start >= last_end:
                merged.append(h)
                last_end = h.end
        hits = merged

        decision = "PASS"
        if any(h.level == "red" for h in hits):
            decision = "BLOCK"
        elif hits:
            decision = "REVIEW"

        # 给你人工审更方便：用原文做上下文片段（粗定位）
        lower = (raw or "").lower()
        snippets = []
        for h in hits:
            idx = lower.find(h.word.lower())
            if idx == -1:
                continue
            L = max(0, idx - 24)
            R = min(len(raw), idx + len(h.word) + 24)
            snippets.append({
                "word": h.word,
                "level": h.level,
                "snippet": raw[L:R]
            })

        return {
            "decision": decision,
            "hits": [{"word": h.word, "level": h.level} for h in hits],
            "snippets": snippets,
        }
```

## Step 3：加 3 个词库文件
放到 `app/` 同级（或你习惯的 `data/` 目录）：

- `words_red.txt`（一行一个强风险词）
- `words_gray.txt`（容易误杀的灰词）
- `whitelist.txt`（白名单短语，覆盖误杀）

## Step 4：在 FastAPI 里加接口（把“检测”落到后端）
在 `app/main.py` 里做两件事：

1) 初始化一个全局 `TextFilter`
2) 增加 `POST /api/review_text`

示例（你按你项目已有的 auth 方式加上依赖校验即可）：

```python
# main.py 顶部附近
from fastapi import Body
from app.text_filter import TextFilter

FILTER = TextFilter(
    red_path="app/words_red.txt",
    gray_path="app/words_gray.txt",
    whitelist_path="app/whitelist.txt",
)

@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: dict = Body(...)):
    # 你项目如果有 “必须登录” 的依赖（比如 get_current_user），这里加上即可
    title = payload.get("title","")
    desc = payload.get("description","")
    subs = payload.get("subtitles","")
    extra = payload.get("extra","")

    merged = "\n".join([title, desc, subs, extra]).strip()

    result = FILTER.scan(merged)

    # code：你可以先简单用时间戳/随机，后续再换成更严格的生成规则
    # 先给你一个可用字段
    import time, secrets
    code = payload.get("code") or f"{int(time.time())}-{secrets.token_hex(3)}"

    return {"code": code, **result}

@app.post("/api/reload_words")
def reload_words():
    # 方便你改词库后立即生效
    FILTER.reload()
    return {"ok": True, "red": len(FILTER.red), "gray": len(FILTER.gray), "white": len(FILTER.white)}
```

> 你之前 main.py 里那套 `normalize_text / contains_banned_names` 可以保留当 fallback，但主路径用 AC 自动机。

---

## Step 5：改 tools.html（删前端模拟，改成真实调用）
核心就是把原来前端“正则 + setTimeout 模拟审核”的代码删掉，换成：

```js
async function review() {
  const payload = {
    title: document.querySelector("#title").value,
    description: document.querySelector("#desc").value,
    subtitles: document.querySelector("#subs").value
  };

  const r = await fetch("/api/review_text", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  const data = await r.json();
  // data.decision / data.hits / data.snippets / data.code
  renderResult(data);
}
```

## 你现在就能得到的效果
- **文本检测是真实后端执行**（不是前端演戏）

OK，现在把 **Gmail 批量邮件（每 10 个 code 一封）** + **Telegram 封面图转发（发完立删）**接进你现有 FastAPI 项目。下面给你“最小可跑”的改法。

我按你最终需求定死规则：

- 文本审核 `/api/review_text`：每次生成 `code`，把结果写入 SQLite 队列

- 邮件和 Telegram 都用同一个 `code` 回溯

# 3) 新增 4 个文件

## A) `app/storage.py`（SQLite 队列）
```python
# app/storage.py
import os, sqlite3, json, time
from typing import Dict, List, Any

def get_db(db_path: str):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(db_path), exist_ok=True) if "/" in db_path else None
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

def init_db(db_path: str):
    conn = get_db(db_path)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS review_queue (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      code TEXT UNIQUE,
      title TEXT,
      raw_text TEXT,
      decision TEXT,
      hits_json TEXT,
      html_section TEXT,
      created_at INTEGER,
      sent_at INTEGER
    )
    """)
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def enqueue(db_path: str, item: Dict[str, Any]):
    conn = get_db(db_path)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
      INSERT OR REPLACE INTO review_queue
      (code,title,raw_text,decision,hits_json,html_section,created_at,sent_at)
      VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,COALESCE((SELECT sent_at FROM review_queue WHERE code=?), NULL))
    """, (
        item["code"],
        item.get("title",""),
        item.get("raw_text",""),
        item.get("decision","PASS"),
        json.dumps(item.get("hits",[]), ensure_ascii=False),
        item.get("html_section",""),
        int(time.time()),
        item["code"],
    ))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def count_unsent(db_path: str) -> int:
    conn = get_db(db_path)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM review_queue WHERE sent_at IS NULL")
    n = cur.fetchone()["c"]
    conn.close()
    return int(n)

def fetch_next_batch(db_path: str, batch_size: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    conn = get_db(db_path)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
      SELECT * FROM review_queue
      WHERE sent_at IS NULL
      ORDER BY created_at ASC
      LIMIT ?
    """, (batch_size,))
    rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]
    conn.close()
    return rows

def mark_sent(db_path: str, codes: List[str]):
    if not codes:
        return
    conn = get_db(db_path)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    now = int(time.time())
    cur.executemany("UPDATE review_queue SET sent_at=? WHERE code=?", [(now, c) for c in codes])
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
```

## B) `app/notifier_gmail.py`（发 Gmail HTML）
```python
# app/notifier_gmail.py
import smtplib
from email.message import EmailMessage

def send_gmail_html(sender: str, app_password: str, to_addr: str, subject: str, html_body: str):
    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = sender
    msg["To"] = to_addr
    msg.set_content("请用支持 HTML 的邮箱客户端查看。")
    msg.add_alternative(html_body, subtype="html")

    with smtplib.SMTP("smtp.gmail.com", 587) as s:
        s.ehlo()
        s.starttls()
        s.login(sender, app_password)
        s.send_message(msg)
```

## C) `app/notifier_tg.py`（发 Telegram 图片）
```python
# app/notifier_tg.py
import requests

def send_telegram_photo(bot_token: str, chat_id: str, image_path: str, caption: str):
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{bot_token}/sendPhoto"
    with open(image_path, "rb") as f:
        files = {"photo": f}
        data = {"chat_id": chat_id, "caption": caption}
        r = requests.post(url, data=data, files=files, timeout=60)
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.json()
```

## D) `app/batch_mailer.py`（每 10 个 code 合并一封）
```python
# app/batch_mailer.py
import os
from typing import List, Dict
from app.storage import count_unsent, fetch_next_batch, mark_sent
from app.notifier_gmail import send_gmail_html

BATCH_SIZE = 10

def wrap_batch_html(items: List[Dict]) -> str:
    # items: rows from DB; each contains code/title/decision/html_section
    toc = "\n".join([
        f"<li><a href='#{it['code']}'>{it['code']}</a> "
        f"<span style='color:#666'>[{it['decision']}]</span> {it.get('title','')[:60]}</li>"
        for it in items
    ])

    sections = "\n".join([
        f"<hr/>"
        f"<h2 id='{it['code']}'>code={it['code']} <span style='color:#666;font-size:12px'>[{it['decision']}]</span></h2>"
        f"<div style='color:#666;margin-bottom:8px;'><b>标题</b>: {it.get('title','')[:120]}</div>"
        f"{it.get('html_section','')}"
        for it in items
    ])

    return f"""<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/></head>
<body style="font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial;padding:24px;line-height:1.55;">
  <h1 style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">审核批次报告（10 合 1）</h1>
  <div style="margin:12px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:12px;">
    <b>目录</b>
    <ol>{toc}</ol>
  </div>
  {sections}
</body></html>"""

def try_send_batch(db_path: str, gmail_sender: str, gmail_pw: str, gmail_to: str) -> Dict:
    n = count_unsent(db_path)
    if n < BATCH_SIZE:
        return {"sent": False, "pending": n}

    batch = fetch_next_batch(db_path, BATCH_SIZE)
    codes = [b["code"] for b in batch]

    html = wrap_batch_html(batch)
    subject = f"[REVIEW BATCH] 10 codes | {codes[0]} … {codes[-1]}"
    send_gmail_html(gmail_sender, gmail_pw, gmail_to, subject, html)

    mark_sent(db_path, codes)
    return {"sent": True, "codes": codes, "pending": count_unsent(db_path)}
```

## A) 在 `/api/review_text` 里：入队 + 满 10 自动发邮件
你在你现有 `review_text` 末尾加“入队 + 触发 batch”：

```python
# 伪代码：你已有 result = FILTER.scan(merged) 和 code
# result: { decision, hits, snippets ... }

# 你这里建议把“高亮 HTML section”也生成出来
# 简化：先只放 snippets + hits；后面你要完全高亮我再给你补成同款 report.py
def build_simple_section(result: dict, raw_text: str) -> str:
    hits = result.get("hits", [])
    snippets = result.get("snippets", [])
    hit_list = "".join([f"<li>[{h['level']}] {h['word']}</li>" for h in hits]) or "<li>无命中</li>"
    sn_list = "".join([f"<div style='margin:8px 0;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:8px;'>"
                       f"<b>[{s['level']}] {s['word']}</b><div style='white-space:pre-wrap;'>{s['snippet']}</div></div>"
                       for s in snippets]) or "<div>无上下文</div>"
    return f"""
      <h3>命中</h3><ul>{hit_list}</ul>
      <h3>上下文</h3>{sn_list}
      <details><summary>展开全文</summary><div style='white-space:pre-wrap;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;'>{raw_text}</div></details>
    """

html_section = build_simple_section(result, merged)

enqueue(DB_PATH, {
  "code": code,
  "title": title,
  "raw_text": merged,
  "decision": result["decision"],
  "hits": result.get("hits", []),
  "html_section": html_section,
})

batch_status = {"sent": False}
if GMAIL_SENDER and GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD and GMAIL_TO:
    batch_status = try_send_batch(DB_PATH, GMAIL_SENDER, GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD, GMAIL_TO)

return {"code": code, **result, "batch": batch_status}
```

这样你每次审核文本都会自动入队，凑够 10 自动发邮件。

## B) 新增 `/api/push_thumbnail`：上传封面图转发 Telegram，转发后立删
```python
from fastapi import UploadFile, File, Form

@app.post("/api/push_thumbnail")
async def push_thumbnail(
    code: str = Form(...),
    title: str = Form(""),
    file: UploadFile = File(...)
):
    # 这里也建议加你已有的登录依赖，避免被人滥用
    if not (TG_TOKEN and TG_CHAT_ID):
        return {"ok": False, "error": "telegram not configured"}

    suffix = os.path.splitext(file.filename or "")[1] or ".jpg"
    tmp_path = None
    try:
        fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=suffix)
        os.close(fd)
        with open(tmp_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(await file.read())

        caption = f"code={code}\n{title[:120]}"
        send_telegram_photo(TG_TOKEN, TG_CHAT_ID, tmp_path, caption)

        return {"ok": True}
    finally:
        if tmp_path and os.path.exists(tmp_path):
            os.remove(tmp_path)
```

这就满足你要求：**图片只做转发，不在服务器保留**。

# 5) 你现在怎么用（最实操）
### 文本
- tools 页面提交文本到 `/api/review_text`
- 后端返回 `code`，并自动入队  
- 队列满 10 自动发一封 Gmail（HTML 正文可读）

### 封面图（可选）
- 你在 tools 页面选图，带上同一个 `code` 调 `/api/push_thumbnail`
- 服务器转发到 Telegram 群，立删本地文件  
- 你在 Telegram 像相册一样翻图，搜 `code` 回溯

app = FastAPI(title="Slice Guard V1.2 (Auth + RedLine Check)")

# ========= 策略与资源加载 =========
POLICY = {}
BANNED_HASHES = []

# 简单内存缓存：同一张图不重复 OCR（MVP够用）
CACHE: Dict[str, Dict] = {}


def load_resources():
    global POLICY, BANNED_HASHES
    print(">>> Loading Policy & Banned Avatars...")

    # 1. Load YAML
    try:
        with open("policy.yml", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            POLICY = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"!!! Error loading policy.yml: {e}")
        POLICY = {"banned_names": [], "phash_threshold": 6}

    # 2. Pre-calculate pHash for banned avatars
    banned_dir = "banned_avatars"
    images = glob.glob(os.path.join(banned_dir, "*.*"))
    count = 0
    for img_path in images:
        try:
            with Image.open(img_path) as img:
                h = imagehash.phash(img.convert("RGB"))
                BANNED_HASHES.append(h)
                count += 1
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipping bad image {img_path}: {e}")

    print(
        f">>> Loaded {count} banned avatar hashes. Threshold: {POLICY.get('phash_threshold', 6)}"
    )


load_resources()

# 初始化审核数据库
init_audit_db()


# ========= db helpers =========
def db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn


def init_db():
    conn = db()
    conn.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        email TEXT UNIQUE,
        password_hash TEXT,
        handle TEXT,
        google_sub TEXT UNIQUE,
        name TEXT,
        picture TEXT,
        verified INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
    );
    """)
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


init_db()


# ========= util helpers =========
def normalize_handle(h: str) -> str:
    h = (h or "").strip()
    if h and not h.startswith("@"):
        h = "@" + h
    return h.lower()


def token_hash(token: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()


def now_ts() -> int:
    return int(time.time())


def create_jwt(user_id: int) -> str:
    return jwt.encode(
        {"sub": str(user_id), "exp": now_ts() + JWT_TTL_DAYS * 86400},
        JWT_SECRET,
        algorithm=JWT_ALG,
    )


def get_current_user(authorization: str = Header(None)):
    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
        raise HTTPException(401, "missing token")
    try:
        uid = int(
            jwt.decode(authorization.split(" ")[1], JWT_SECRET, algorithms=[JWT_ALG])[
                "sub"
            ]
        )
    except:
        raise HTTPException(401, "bad token")
    conn = db()
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (uid,)).fetchone()
    conn.close()
    if not row or row["verified"] != 1:
        raise HTTPException(403, "unverified or not found")
    return dict(row)


# ========= detection logic =========


def check_text_blacklist(text: str) -> bool:
    """如果命中黑名单返回 True (表示违规)"""
    if not text:
        return False
    lower = text.lower()

    # 检查禁止名称
    for name in POLICY.get("banned_names", []):
        if name.lower() in lower:
            return True

    # 检查禁止词汇
    for term in POLICY.get("banned_terms", []):
        if term.lower() in lower:
            return True

    return False


def check_image_similarity(img: Image.Image) -> bool:
    """如果图片与 banned_avatars 太像，返回 True (表示违规)"""
    if not BANNED_HASHES:
        return False

    try:
        # 预处理：调整图片大小，提高检测一致性
        img = img.convert("RGB")
        img.thumbnail((512, 512))  # 缩放到合适大小

        # 使用多种哈希算法提高检测准确性
        user_phash = imagehash.phash(img)
        user_dhash = imagehash.dhash(img)
        user_whash = imagehash.whash(img)

        threshold = POLICY.get("phash_threshold", 6)
        dhash_threshold = threshold * 1.5  # dhash 阈值稍高
        whash_threshold = threshold * 1.2  # whash 阈值适中

        for i, banned_h in enumerate(BANNED_HASHES):
            # 检查 pHash
            phash_distance = user_phash - banned_h
            if phash_distance <= threshold:
                print(
                    f"pHash match found! Index: {i}, Dist: {phash_distance}, Threshold: {threshold}"
                )
                return True

            # 检查 dHash
            dhash_distance = user_dhash - banned_h
            if dhash_distance <= dhash_threshold:
                print(
                    f"dHash match found! Index: {i}, Dist: {dhash_distance}, Threshold: {dhash_threshold}"
                )
                return True

            # 检查 wHash
            whash_distance = user_whash - banned_h
            if whash_distance <= whash_threshold:
                print(
                    f"wHash match found! Index: {i}, Dist: {whash_distance}, Threshold: {whash_threshold}"
                )
                return True
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Hash check error: {e}")

    return False


def safe_ocr(img: Image.Image) -> str:
    try:
        return pytesseract.image_to_string(img, lang="eng+chi_sim") or ""
    except:
        return ""


def make_approve_code(handle: str, *args) -> str:
    payload = f"{handle}|" + "|".join(str(a) for a in args)
    digest = hmac.new(APP_SECRET.encode(), payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256).digest()

    n = int.from_bytes(digest, "big")
    chars = []
    for _ in range(8):
        n, r = divmod(n, 32)
        chars.append(ALPHABET[r])
    raw = "".join(reversed(chars))
    return f"{raw[:4]}-{raw[4:]}"


# ========= schemas =========
class AuthIn(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str = Field(min_length=8)
    handle: str = None


class ResetIn(BaseModel):
    token: str
    new_password: str


class GoogleLoginIn(BaseModel):
    credential: str


class ReviewTextIn(BaseModel):
    title: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    subtitles: str = ""
    extra: str = ""
    code: str | None = None


# ========= static files =========
app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory="static"), name="static")


# ========= endpoints =========
@app.get("/")
def root():
    return FileResponse("static/index.html")


@app.post("/auth/login")
def login(body: AuthIn):
    conn = db()
    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email=?", (body.email,)).fetchone()
    conn.close()
    if (
        not row
        or not pwd_context.verify(body.password, row["password_hash"])
        or not row["verified"]
    ):
        raise HTTPException(401, "fail")
    return {"token": create_jwt(row["id"]), "handle": row["handle"]}


@app.post("/auth/google")
def auth_google(data: GoogleLoginIn):
    if not GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:
        raise HTTPException(500, "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID not set")

    # 验证 Google ID Token
    try:
        idinfo = id_token.verify_oauth2_token(
            data.credential, grequests.Request(), GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID
        )
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid Google credential")

    # 提取用户信息
    sub = idinfo.get("sub")
    email = idinfo.get("email")
    name = idinfo.get("name")
    picture = idinfo.get("picture")

    if not sub:
        raise HTTPException(400, "Missing sub")

    # 在数据库中查找或创建用户记录
    conn = db()
    cur = conn.execute("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE google_sub=?", (sub,))
    row = cur.fetchone()
    if row:
        user_id = row["id"]
        handle = row["handle"]
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE users SET email=?, name=?, picture=? WHERE id=?",
            (email, name, picture, user_id),
        )
    else:
        # 创建新用户
        handle = f"@{name.replace(' ', '_').lower()}" if name else f"@user_{sub[:8]}"
        conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users (google_sub, email, name, picture, handle, verified, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1, ?)",
            (sub, email, name, picture, handle, int(time.time())),
        )
        user_id = conn.execute(
            "SELECT id FROM users WHERE google_sub=?", (sub,)
        ).fetchone()["id"]
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

    # 签发 JWT 令牌
    return {"token": create_jwt(user_id), "handle": handle}


# ========= REVIEW =========
@app.post("/review")
async def review(
    title: str = Form(...),
    description: str = Form(""),
    tags: str = Form(""),
    subtitles: UploadFile | None = File(None),
    thumbnail: UploadFile = File(...),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    handle_norm = normalize_handle(user["handle"])

    # --- 1. 硬性安全检查 ---

    # 检查 A: 文件类型
    if thumbnail.content_type not in ALLOWED_TYPES:
        print(f"Reject: Bad content type {thumbnail.content_type}")
        return {"result": "NO"}

    # 检查 B: 读取文件并检查大小
    thumb_bytes = await thumbnail.read()

    if len(thumb_bytes) > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
        print(f"Reject: File too large ({len(thumb_bytes)} bytes)")
        return {"result": "NO"}

    # 检查 C: 图片是否损坏
    try:
        img = Image.open(BytesIO(thumb_bytes)).convert("RGB")
        img.thumbnail((1920, 1080))
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Reject: Invalid image file. {e}")
        return {"result": "NO"}

    # --- 2. 业务逻辑检查 ---

    # 读取字幕文本
    subtitles_text = ""
    if subtitles:
        sub_bytes = await subtitles.read()
        if len(sub_bytes) > 1 * 1024 * 1024:
            return {"result": "NO"}
        subtitles_text = sub_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

    # OCR 提取文字
    ocr_text = safe_ocr(img)

    # 文本黑名单检查
    full_text_check = [title, description, tags, subtitles_text, ocr_text]
    for text in full_text_check:
        if check_text_blacklist(text):
            print(f"Text Ban Hit: {text[:20]}...")
            return {"result": "NO"}

    # 图片 pHash 相似度检查
    if check_image_similarity(img):
        print("Image Similarity Hit")
        return {"result": "NO"}

    # --- 3.通过 ---
    code = make_approve_code(
        handle_norm, title, description, tags, subtitles_text, ocr_text
    )
    return {"result": "OK", "code": code}


# ========= TEST ENDPOINT (不需要认证) =========
@app.post("/test-detect")
async def test_detect(
    request: Request,
):
    """测试检测端点 - 不需要认证"""
    try:
        # 解析表单数据
        form_data = await request.form()

        # 获取表单字段
        title = form_data.get("title", "")
        description = form_data.get("description", "")
        subtitles = form_data.get("subtitles", "")

        # 检查是否有图片文件
        image_file = None
        if "image_file" in form_data:
            image_file = form_data["image_file"]

        # 收集所有错误
        errors = []

        # --- 1. 文本检测 ---
        # 检查标题
        if title and check_text_blacklist(title):
            print(f"Text Ban Hit in title: {title[:20]}...")
            errors.append("标题包含敏感内容")

        # 检查说明
        if description and check_text_blacklist(description):
            print(f"Text Ban Hit in description: {description[:20]}...")
            errors.append("说明包含敏感内容")

        # 检查字幕
        if subtitles and check_text_blacklist(subtitles):
            print(f"Text Ban Hit in subtitles: {subtitles[:20]}...")
            errors.append("字幕包含敏感内容")

        # --- 2. 图片检测 (如果有) ---
        if image_file:
            # 检查 A: 文件类型
            if image_file.content_type not in ALLOWED_TYPES:
                print(f"Reject: Bad content type {image_file.content_type}")
                errors.append("图片类型不支持")
            else:
                # 检查 B: 读取文件并检查大小
                img_bytes = await image_file.read()

                if len(img_bytes) > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
                    print(f"Reject: File too large ({len(img_bytes)} bytes)")
                    errors.append("图片文件过大")
                else:
                    # 检查 C: 图片是否损坏
                    try:
                        img = Image.open(BytesIO(img_bytes)).convert("RGB")
                        img.thumbnail((1920, 1080))

                        # 检查 D: 图片相似度
                        if check_image_similarity(img):
                            print("Image Similarity Hit")
                            errors.append("图片包含敏感内容")
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Reject: Invalid image file. {e}")
                        errors.append("图片文件损坏")

        # --- 3. 检查结果 ---
        if errors:
            # 构建错误信息
            error_message = "检测不通过，错误：" + "、".join(errors)
            return {"result": "NO", "reason": error_message}

        # --- 4.通过 ---
        # 生成验证码，使用测试用户标识
        test_handle = "@test_user"
        code = make_approve_code(test_handle, title, description, subtitles)
        return {"result": "OK", "reason": "内容安全", "code": code}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error in test-detect: {e}")
        return {"result": "NO", "reason": f"服务器错误: {str(e)}"}


# ========= /api/review ENDPOINT =========
@app.post("/api/review")
async def review(
    title: str = Form(""),
    description: str = Form(""),
    tags: str = Form(""),
    subtitles: str = Form(""),
    thumbnail: UploadFile = File(...),
):
    img_bytes = await thumbnail.read()
    img_sha = sha256_hex(img_bytes)

    if img_sha in CACHE:
        result = CACHE[img_sha]
    else:
        result = audit_thumbnail(
            img_bytes,
            conf_threshold_no=0.75,
            title=title,
            description=description,
            tags=tags,
            subtitles=subtitles,
        )
        CACHE[img_sha] = result

    # 写日志
    row = {
        "created_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "image_sha256": img_sha,
        "decision": result["result"],
        "review": 1 if result.get("review") else 0,
        "ocr_conf": float(result.get("ocr_conf", 0.0)),
        "hits": json.dumps(result.get("hits", []), ensure_ascii=False)
        if "hits" in result
        else None,
        "ocr_text": result.get("ocr_text"),
    }
    insert_log(row)

    # 对外保持两态：NO / OK(+code)
    if result["result"] == "NO":
        return JSONResponse(
            {
                "result": "NO",
                "review": bool(result.get("review", False)),  # 你内部可以据此走复核队列
                "reasons": result.get("reasons", []),
                "hits": result.get("hits", []),
                "ocr_conf": result.get("ocr_conf", 0.0),
            }
        )
    else:
        return JSONResponse(
            {
                "result": "OK",
                "code": result["code"],
                "ocr_conf": result.get("ocr_conf", 0.0),
            }
        )


@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: ReviewTextIn):
    merged = "\n".join(
        [payload.title, payload.description, payload.subtitles, payload.extra]
    ).strip()
    result = TEXT_FILTER.scan(merged)

    code = payload.code
    if not code:
        code = f"{int(time.time())}-{secrets.token_hex(3)}"

    return {"code": code, **result}


@app.post("/api/reload_words")
def reload_words():
    TEXT_FILTER.reload()
    return {
        "ok": True,
        "red": len(TEXT_FILTER.red),
        "gray": len(TEXT_FILTER.gray),
        "white": len(TEXT_FILTER.white),
    }

## 4) Gmail 发送 + HTML 组装（直接贴）
放在 `util helpers` 后面（任意位置都行）：

```python
def gmail_send_html(subject: str, html_body: str):
    if not (GMAIL_SENDER and GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD and GMAIL_TO):
        return False

    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = GMAIL_SENDER
    msg["To"] = GMAIL_TO
    msg.set_content("请用支持 HTML 的邮箱客户端查看。")
    msg.add_alternative(html_body, subtype="html")

    with smtplib.SMTP("smtp.gmail.com", 587) as s:
        s.ehlo()
        s.starttls()
        s.login(GMAIL_SENDER, GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD)
        s.send_message(msg)
    return True


def highlight_html(raw_text: str) -> str:
    # 先 escape，再用简单替换高亮（足够你人工审核）
    safe = html.escape(raw_text or "", quote=True)

    # 灰词先高亮
    for w in sorted(set(TEXT_FILTER.gray), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w:
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(html.escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#fff3bf;">{html.escape(w)}</mark>')

    # 红词再高亮
    for w in sorted(set(TEXT_FILTER.red), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w:
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(html.escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#ffd6d6;">{html.escape(w)}</mark>')

    return safe


def build_html_section(code: str, title: str, merged: str, result: dict) -> str:
    hits = result.get("hits", [])
    snippets = result.get("snippets", [])
    decision = result.get("decision", "PASS")

    hit_rows = ""
    if hits:
        for i, h in enumerate(hits, 1):
            hit_rows += f"<tr><td>{i}</td><td>{html.escape(h.get('level',''))}</td><td>{html.escape(h.get('word',''))}</td></tr>"
    else:
        hit_rows = "<tr><td colspan='3'>无命中</td></tr>"

    sn_block = ""
    if snippets:
        for s in snippets:
            sn_block += (
                "<div style='margin:8px 0;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:10px;'>"
                f"<b>[{html.escape(s.get('level',''))}] {html.escape(s.get('word',''))}</b>"
                f"<div style='white-space:pre-wrap;margin-top:6px;font-family:ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,monospace;'>"
                f"{html.escape(s.get('snippet',''))}"
                "</div></div>"
            )
    else:
        sn_block = "<div>无上下文</div>"

    full = highlight_html(merged)

    return f"""
    <div style="color:#666;margin:6px 0 12px 0;">
      <div><b>code</b>: {html.escape(code)}</div>
      <div><b>标题</b>: {html.escape(title or "")}</div>
      <div><b>判定</b>: <span style="padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid #ddd;">{html.escape(decision)}</span></div>
    </div>

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">命中总览</h3>
    <table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">#</th>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">level</th>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">word</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>{hit_rows}</tbody>
    </table>

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">命中上下文</h3>
    {sn_block}

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">全文（高亮）</h3>
    <div style="white-space:pre-wrap;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;">
      {full}
    </div>
    """


def mail_queue_enqueue(code: str, title: str, merged: str, result: dict):
    decision = result.get("decision", "PASS")
    section = build_html_section(code, title, merged, result)

    conn = db()
    conn.execute("""
        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO mail_queue
        (code, title, merged, decision, hits_json, html_section, created_at, sent_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE((SELECT sent_at FROM mail_queue WHERE code=?), NULL))
    """, (
        code,
        title or "",
        merged or "",
        decision,
        json.dumps(result.get("hits", []), ensure_ascii=False),
        section,
        now_ts(),
        code,
    ))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


def mail_queue_pending_count() -> int:
    conn = db()
    n = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM mail_queue WHERE sent_at IS NULL").fetchone()["c"]
    conn.close()
    return int(n)


def mail_queue_fetch_batch(n: int):
    conn = db()
    rows = conn.execute("""
        SELECT * FROM mail_queue
        WHERE sent_at IS NULL
        ORDER BY created_at ASC
        LIMIT ?
    """, (n,)).fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [dict(r) for r in rows]


def mail_queue_mark_sent(codes):
    if not codes:
        return
    conn = db()
    ts = now_ts()
    conn.executemany("UPDATE mail_queue SET sent_at=? WHERE code=?", [(ts, c) for c in codes])
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


def wrap_batch_email_html(items):
    toc = "".join([
        f"<li><a href='#{it['code']}'>{html.escape(it['code'])}</a> "
        f"<span style='color:#666'>[{html.escape(it.get('decision',''))}]</span> "
        f"{html.escape((it.get('title') or '')[:60])}</li>"
        for it in items
    ])

    sections = ""
    for it in items:
        sections += (
            f"<hr style='border:none;border-top:1px solid #eee;margin:18px 0;'/>"
            f"<h2 id='{html.escape(it['code'])}' style='margin:0 0 8px 0;'>code={html.escape(it['code'])}</h2>"
            f"{it.get('html_section','')}"
        )

    return f"""<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/></head>
<body style="font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial;padding:24px;line-height:1.55;">
  <h1 style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">审核批次报告（10 合 1）</h1>
  <div style="margin:12px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:12px;">
    <b>目录</b>
    <ol>{toc}</ol>
  </div>
  {sections}
</body></html>"""


def try_send_batch_if_ready():
    pending = mail_queue_pending_count()
    if pending < BATCH_SIZE:
        return {"sent": False, "pending": pending}

    items = mail_queue_fetch_batch(BATCH_SIZE)
    codes = [it["code"] for it in items]

    subject = f"[REVIEW BATCH] 10 codes | {codes[0]} … {codes[-1]}"
    body = wrap_batch_email_html(items)

    ok = gmail_send_html(subject, body)
    if ok:
        mail_queue_mark_sent(codes)
        return {"sent": True, "codes": codes, "pending": mail_queue_pending_count()}

    return {"sent": False, "pending": pending, "error": "gmail_send_failed"}
```

## 5) 修改 `/api/review_text`：入队 + 满 10 自动发
你现在的 `/api/review_text` 没有鉴权。我建议直接加鉴权，不然任何人都能刷你的 Gmail。

把函数签名改成这样（加 `user=Depends(get_current_user)`）：

```python
@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: ReviewTextIn, user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    merged = "\n".join(
        [payload.title, payload.description, payload.subtitles, payload.extra]
    ).strip()
    result = TEXT_FILTER.scan(merged)

    code = payload.code
    if not code:
        code = f"{int(time.time())}-{secrets.token_hex(3)}"

    # 入队（给 batch 邮件用）
    mail_queue_enqueue(code, payload.title, merged, result)

    # 满 10 自动发一封
    batch = try_send_batch_if_ready()

    return {"code": code, **result, "batch": batch}
```

---

## 6) Telegram：新增一个“只转发封面图，发完立删”的接口
加这个 endpoint（同样建议鉴权）：

```python
@app.post("/api/push_thumbnail")
async def push_thumbnail(
    code: str = Form(...),
    title: str = Form(""),
    thumbnail: UploadFile = File(...),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if not (TG_TOKEN and TG_CHAT_ID):
        raise HTTPException(500, "telegram not configured")

    if thumbnail.content_type not in ALLOWED_TYPES:
        raise HTTPException(400, "bad content type")

    img_bytes = await thumbnail.read()
    if len(img_bytes) > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
        raise HTTPException(413, "file too large")

    tmp_path = None
    try:
        suffix = os.path.splitext(thumbnail.filename or "")[1] or ".jpg"
        fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=suffix)
        os.close(fd)
        with open(tmp_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(img_bytes)

        caption = f"code={code}\n{(title or '')[:120]}"
        url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
        with open(tmp_path, "rb") as f:
            files = {"photo": f}
            data = {"chat_id": TG_CHAT_ID, "caption": caption}
            r = requests.post(url, data=data, files=files, timeout=60)
        r.raise_for_status()

        return {"ok": True}
    finally:
        if tmp_path and os.path.exists(tmp_path):
            os.remove(tmp_path)
```

这满足你要求：**服务器不保存封面图，只负责转发到 Telegram**。

## 部署前必备清单（别跳）
1) VPS 上准备 `.env`（别提交到 git）

Ubuntu/Debian：

```bash
apt install -y python3 python3-venv python3-pip \
  tesseract-ocr tesseract-ocr-eng tesseract-ocr-chi-sim \

```

## 方案 A：Docker（最省心，推荐）
### 1) 写一个 docker-compose.yml（核心是挂载 data 目录 + 传 env）
```yaml
services:
  app:
    build: .
    container_name: slice-guard
    restart: always
    env_file:
      - .env
    volumes:
      - ./data:/app/data
    ports:
      - "8787:8787"
```

### 2) Dockerfile（如果你还没有）
关键：装 tesseract + 语言包
```dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
  tesseract-ocr tesseract-ocr-eng tesseract-ocr-chi-sim \
  libgl1 \
  && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

WORKDIR /app
COPY . /app
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r app/requirements.txt

EXPOSE 8787
CMD ["uvicorn", "app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8787"]
```

### 3) 启动
```bash
docker compose up -d --build
docker logs -f slice-guard
```

> SQLite 会落在宿主机 `./data`，容器重启不丢。

---

## 方案 B：systemd（不用 Docker）
### 1) 拉代码 + 建虚拟环境
```bash
cd /root
git clone 你的仓库 slice-guard
cd slice-guard
python3 -m venv .venv
source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r app/requirements.txt
```

### 2) 写环境文件 `/root/slice-guard/.env`
把你的变量都放进去（同本地）。

### 3) systemd 服务
`/etc/systemd/system/slice-guard.service`
```ini
[Unit]
Description=Slice Guard API
After=network.target

[Service]
WorkingDirectory=/root/slice-guard
EnvironmentFile=/root/slice-guard/.env
ExecStart=/root/slice-guard/.venv/bin/uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8787
Restart=always
RestartSec=2

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

启动：
```bash
systemctl daemon-reload
systemctl enable --now slice-guard
journalctl -u slice-guard -f
```

---

## 反代（Nginx）要点
你上传图片走 multipart，注意 Nginx 默认可能卡 body 大小。你现在后端限制 2MB，但 Nginx 也要放行，例如：
```nginx
client_max_body_size 5m;
```

## 3) 加一个邮件队列表（复用你现有 SQLite DB_PATH）

```python
def init_mail_queue():
    conn = db()
    conn.execute("""
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mail_queue (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        code TEXT UNIQUE,
        title TEXT,
        merged TEXT,
        decision TEXT,
        hits_json TEXT,
        html_section TEXT,
        created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
        sent_at INTEGER
    );
    """)
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

init_mail_queue()
```

## 4) 加 Gmail 发送 + 批量 HTML 组装 + 队列操作
把下面整段放到 `util helpers` 后面（任意位置都行）：

```python
def gmail_send_html(subject: str, html_body: str):
    if not (GMAIL_SENDER and GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD and GMAIL_TO):
        return False

    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["From"] = GMAIL_SENDER
    msg["To"] = GMAIL_TO
    msg.set_content("请用支持 HTML 的邮箱客户端查看。")
    msg.add_alternative(html_body, subtype="html")

    with smtplib.SMTP("smtp.gmail.com", 587) as s:
        s.ehlo()
        s.starttls()
        s.login(GMAIL_SENDER, GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD)
        s.send_message(msg)
    return True


def highlight_html(raw_text: str) -> str:
    safe = html.escape(raw_text or "", quote=True)

    # 灰词先高亮
    for w in sorted(set(TEXT_FILTER.gray), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w:
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(html.escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#fff3bf;">{html.escape(w)}</mark>')

    # 红词再高亮
    for w in sorted(set(TEXT_FILTER.red), key=len, reverse=True):
        if not w:
            continue
        safe = safe.replace(html.escape(w), f'<mark style="background:#ffd6d6;">{html.escape(w)}</mark>')

    return safe


def build_html_section(code: str, title: str, merged: str, result: dict) -> str:
    hits = result.get("hits", [])
    snippets = result.get("snippets", [])
    decision = result.get("decision", "PASS")

    hit_rows = ""
    if hits:
        for i, h in enumerate(hits, 1):
            hit_rows += f"<tr><td>{i}</td><td>{html.escape(h.get('level',''))}</td><td>{html.escape(h.get('word',''))}</td></tr>"
    else:
        hit_rows = "<tr><td colspan='3'>无命中</td></tr>"

    sn_block = ""
    if snippets:
        for s in snippets:
            sn_block += (
                "<div style='margin:8px 0;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:10px;'>"
                f"<b>[{html.escape(s.get('level',''))}] {html.escape(s.get('word',''))}</b>"
                f"<div style='white-space:pre-wrap;margin-top:6px;font-family:ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,monospace;'>"
                f"{html.escape(s.get('snippet',''))}"
                "</div></div>"
            )
    else:
        sn_block = "<div>无上下文</div>"

    full = highlight_html(merged)

    return f"""
    <div style="color:#666;margin:6px 0 12px 0;">
      <div><b>code</b>: {html.escape(code)}</div>
      <div><b>标题</b>: {html.escape(title or "")}</div>
      <div><b>判定</b>: <span style="padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid #ddd;">{html.escape(decision)}</span></div>
    </div>

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">命中总览</h3>
    <table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">#</th>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">level</th>
          <th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:6px;">word</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>{hit_rows}</tbody>
    </table>

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">命中上下文</h3>
    {sn_block}

    <h3 style="margin:14px 0 6px 0;">全文（高亮）</h3>
    <div style="white-space:pre-wrap;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:12px;padding:12px;">
      {full}
    </div>
    """


def mail_queue_enqueue(code: str, title: str, merged: str, result: dict):
    decision = result.get("decision", "PASS")
    section = build_html_section(code, title, merged, result)

    conn = db()
    conn.execute("""
        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO mail_queue
        (code, title, merged, decision, hits_json, html_section, created_at, sent_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE((SELECT sent_at FROM mail_queue WHERE code=?), NULL))
    """, (
        code,
        title or "",
        merged or "",
        decision,
        json.dumps(result.get("hits", []), ensure_ascii=False),
        section,
        now_ts(),
        code,
    ))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


def mail_queue_pending_count() -> int:
    conn = db()
    n = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) c FROM mail_queue WHERE sent_at IS NULL").fetchone()["c"]
    conn.close()
    return int(n)


def mail_queue_fetch_batch(n: int):
    conn = db()
    rows = conn.execute("""
        SELECT * FROM mail_queue
        WHERE sent_at IS NULL
        ORDER BY created_at ASC
        LIMIT ?
    """, (n,)).fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [dict(r) for r in rows]


def mail_queue_mark_sent(codes):
    if not codes:
        return
    conn = db()
    ts = now_ts()
    conn.executemany("UPDATE mail_queue SET sent_at=? WHERE code=?", [(ts, c) for c in codes])
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


def wrap_batch_email_html(items):
    toc = "".join([
        f"<li><a href='#{html.escape(it['code'])}'>{html.escape(it['code'])}</a> "
        f"<span style='color:#666'>[{html.escape(it.get('decision',''))}]</span> "
        f"{html.escape((it.get('title') or '')[:60])}</li>"
        for it in items
    ])

    sections = ""
    for it in items:
        sections += (
            "<hr style='border:none;border-top:1px solid #eee;margin:18px 0;'/>"
            f"<h2 id='{html.escape(it['code'])}' style='margin:0 0 8px 0;'>code={html.escape(it['code'])}</h2>"
            f"{it.get('html_section','')}"
        )

    return f"""<!doctype html>
<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"/></head>
<body style="font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,Helvetica,Arial;padding:24px;line-height:1.55;">
  <h1 style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">审核批次报告（10 合 1）</h1>
  <div style="margin:12px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:12px;">
    <b>目录</b>
    <ol>{toc}</ol>
  </div>
  {sections}
</body></html>"""


def try_send_batch_if_ready():
    pending = mail_queue_pending_count()
    if pending < BATCH_SIZE:
        return {"sent": False, "pending": pending}

    items = mail_queue_fetch_batch(BATCH_SIZE)
    codes = [it["code"] for it in items]

    subject = f"[REVIEW BATCH] 10 codes | {codes[0]} … {codes[-1]}"
    body = wrap_batch_email_html(items)

    ok = gmail_send_html(subject, body)
    if ok:
        mail_queue_mark_sent(codes)
        return {"sent": True, "codes": codes, "pending": mail_queue_pending_count()}

    return {"sent": False, "pending": pending, "error": "gmail_send_failed"}
```

---

## 5) 改 `/api/review_text`：入队 + 满 10 自动发
你现在的 `/api/review_text` 没鉴权。为了防刷爆邮箱，建议加 `Depends(get_current_user)`。

把它改成：

```python
@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: ReviewTextIn, user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    merged = "\n".join(
        [payload.title, payload.description, payload.subtitles, payload.extra]
    ).strip()
    result = TEXT_FILTER.scan(merged)

    code = payload.code
    if not code:
        code = f"{int(time.time())}-{secrets.token_hex(3)}"

    # 入队
    mail_queue_enqueue(code, payload.title, merged, result)

    # 满 10 自动发一封
    batch = try_send_batch_if_ready()

    return {"code": code, **result, "batch": batch}
```

---

## 6) Telegram 补一个“只负责转发封面图”的接口（可选）
你不想改你现有 `/review` 和 `/api/review` 的逻辑，那就加一个独立接口最干净。

```python
@app.post("/api/push_thumbnail")
async def push_thumbnail(
    code: str = Form(...),
    title: str = Form(""),
    thumbnail: UploadFile = File(...),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if not (TG_TOKEN and TG_CHAT_ID):
        raise HTTPException(500, "telegram not configured")

    if thumbnail.content_type not in ALLOWED_TYPES:
        raise HTTPException(400, "bad content type")

    img_bytes = await thumbnail.read()
    if len(img_bytes) > MAX_FILE_SIZE:
        raise HTTPException(413, "file too large")

    tmp_path = None
    try:
        suffix = os.path.splitext(thumbnail.filename or "")[1] or ".jpg"
        fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=suffix)
        os.close(fd)
        with open(tmp_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(img_bytes)

        caption = f"code={code}\n{(title or '')[:120]}"
        url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
        with open(tmp_path, "rb") as f:
            files = {"photo": f}
            data = {"chat_id": TG_CHAT_ID, "caption": caption}
            r = requests.post(url, data=data, files=files, timeout=60)
        r.raise_for_status()

        return {"ok": True}
    finally:
        if tmp_path and os.path.exists(tmp_path):
            os.remove(tmp_path)
```

这样 Telegram 就纯相册用途，服务器不留图，回溯靠 `code`。

你图里这些 env 变量，分别这样“拿到/生成”：

---

## 1) Gmail：`GMAIL_SENDER / GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD / GMAIL_TO` 怎么得到

### `GMAIL_SENDER`
就是你的 Gmail 地址，例如：
- `yourname@gmail.com`

### `GMAIL_TO`
收件人邮箱（可以就是你自己同一个邮箱）：
- `yourname@gmail.com`

### `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD`（关键）
这是 **Gmail 的 App Password（应用专用密码）**，不是你登录密码。

步骤：
1. 进你的 Google 账号安全设置  
2. 开启 **两步验证（2-Step Verification）**
3. 然后在 **App passwords（应用专用密码）** 里生成一个
4. 选择 App: Mail（或 Other），设备随便填个名字
5. 得到一串 **16 位**字符（通常显示为 `xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx`）

把这 16 位填进 `GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD`。

> 如果你看不到 “App passwords”，通常是：没开两步验证，或账号被策略限制（比如某些企业策略）。

---

## 2) Telegram：`TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN / TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` 怎么得到

### `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`
1. 在 Telegram 搜 **@BotFather**
2. `/newbot`
3. 设置 bot 名称和 username
4. BotFather 会返回一串 token：  
   形如 `123456789:AA...`

把它填 `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`。

### `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`
这是你要投递的“群/频道”的 id。

**最稳方法（不用你写代码）：**
1. 把机器人拉进你的目标群/频道（频道要给它发消息权限）
2. 再把 **@RawDataBot** 拉进群里（或任何“显示 chat id”的 bot）
3. 在群里发一句话
4. @RawDataBot 会回一段 JSON，你找：
   - `"chat":{"id": -100xxxxxxxxxx, ...}`  
这个 `id` 就是 `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID`。

> 群一般是负数；频道通常是 `-100...` 开头。

**备选方法（用 Telegram API getUpdates）：**
1. 先给机器人私聊发一句 “hi”，再在群里发一句 “hi”
2. 浏览器打开（把 token 换成你的）：
   - `https://api.telegram.org/bot<YOUR_TOKEN>/getUpdates`
3. 在返回 JSON 里找 `chat.id`

> 注意：如果 bot 开了 privacy mode，它可能看不到群消息。解决：@BotFather -> `/setprivacy` -> 选你的 bot -> `Disable`。  
（但你只是“发图”，其实不需要读群消息，读消息只是为了拿 chat_id 一次。）

---

## 3) 在 VPS Docker 里怎么配置这些 env

### 方法 A：docker-compose + `.env`（推荐）
在 `docker-compose.yml`：
```yaml
services:
  app:
    env_file:
      - .env
    restart: always
```

在同目录建 `.env`：
```bash
GMAIL_SENDER=yourname@gmail.com
GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD=xxxx xxxx xxxx xxxx
GMAIL_TO=yourname@gmail.com

TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=123456789:AA....
TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID=-1001234567890
```

改完重启：
```bash
docker compose up -d
# 或
docker compose restart
```

### 方法 B：直接 docker run 传 -e
```bash
docker run -d \
  -e GMAIL_SENDER=... \
  -e GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD=... \
  -e GMAIL_TO=... \
  -e TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN=... \
  -e TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID=... \
  your_image
```

按你截图里那 6 类问题，我给你一套“最小改动、能直接上生产”的修复补丁（FastAPI + SQLite + 你现有 main.py 结构不大改）。

## 1) 严重：默认密钥 `change_me*` 直接阻断启动

把 `APP_SECRET/JWT_SECRET` **取消默认值**，启动时校验，发现是缺失或 `change_me` 就直接 `raise`。

把下面这段放到 `app/main.py` 顶部（读取 env 后）：

```python
import os

def _must_env(name: str) -> str:
    v = os.getenv(name, "").strip()
    if not v:
        raise RuntimeError(f"[FATAL] env {name} is missing")
    bad = {"change_me", "change_me*", "changeme", "default", "secret"}
    if v.lower() in bad:
        raise RuntimeError(f"[FATAL] env {name} is insecure (placeholder)")
    if len(v) < 24:
        raise RuntimeError(f"[FATAL] env {name} too short (<24)")
    return v

APP_SECRET = _must_env("APP_SECRET")
JWT_SECRET = _must_env("JWT_SECRET")
```

---

## 2) 严重：高消耗接口未登录可刷爆 CPU/DB

把 `/api/review`、`/test-detect`（以及你实际跑 OCR/写库的接口）统一加登录依赖：

```python
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException

# 你已有的：get_current_user()
# def get_current_user(...): ...

def require_user(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="unauthorized")
    return user
```

然后给接口加依赖：

```python
@app.post("/api/review")
def review(payload: dict, user=Depends(require_user)):
    ...

@app.post("/test-detect")
def test_detect(payload: dict, user=Depends(require_user)):
    ...
```

**可选（更稳）**：生产环境直接干掉测试口，只留本地：

```python
IS_PROD = os.getenv("ENV", "dev").lower() == "prod"

@app.post("/test-detect")
def test_detect(payload: dict, user=Depends(require_user)):
    if IS_PROD:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="not found")
    ...
```

---

## 3) 高：邮件发送别卡在请求线程（BackgroundTasks）

把 `/api/review_text` 里同步调用 `try_send_batch_if_ready()` 改成后台任务：

```python
from fastapi import BackgroundTasks

@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: dict, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks, user=Depends(require_user)):
    # ... 你原来的逻辑：写 log / 入队 mail_queue 等

    # 原来：try_send_batch_if_ready()
    background_tasks.add_task(try_send_batch_if_ready)

    return {"ok": True}
```

这能立刻把 “SMTP 慢导致接口卡死/超时” 砍掉。

---

## 4) 中：SQLite 并发锁（WAL + busy_timeout）

你只要在**每次拿到连接后**执行 PRAGMA（最小改动，不换库）：

```python
import sqlite3

DB_PATH = os.getenv("DB_PATH", "data/app.db")

def get_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=30, check_same_thread=False)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;")  # 5s 等锁
    try:
        yield conn
    finally:
        conn.close()
```

你的路由里如果是自己 `sqlite3.connect()`，改成用 `Depends(get_db)`：

```python
from fastapi import Depends

@app.post("/api/review_text")
def review_text(payload: dict, background_tasks: BackgroundTasks, db=Depends(get_db), user=Depends(require_user)):
    # 用 db.execute(...)
    ...
```

---

## 5) 中：内存 CACHE 无上限（改成 LRU 上限）

最小做法：用 `OrderedDict` 做 LRU，上限 N 条（比如 5000）：

```python
from collections import OrderedDict

CACHE_MAX = int(os.getenv("CACHE_MAX", "5000"))
CACHE = OrderedDict()

def cache_get(k):
    v = CACHE.get(k)
    if v is not None:
        CACHE.move_to_end(k)
    return v

def cache_set(k, v):
    CACHE[k] = v
    CACHE.move_to_end(k)
    while len(CACHE) > CACHE_MAX:
        CACHE.popitem(last=False)
```

把你原来直接 `CACHE[key]=...` 的地方替换成 `cache_set()`，读取用 `cache_get()`。

---

## 6) 中：上传文件 suffix 来自用户文件名（只允许 jpg/jpeg/png）

你截图里说你用 `mkstemp` 是安全的，但 suffix 不该信用户。改成“读头判断类型 → 自己决定后缀”。

```python
import imghdr
import tempfile
from fastapi import UploadFile, File, HTTPException

ALLOWED = {"jpeg": ".jpg", "png": ".png"}

def _sniff_ext(data: bytes) -> str:
    kind = imghdr.what(None, h=data)
    if kind == "jpg":
        kind = "jpeg"
    if kind not in ALLOWED:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="only jpg/jpeg/png allowed")
    return ALLOWED[kind]

@app.post("/api/push_thumbnail")
async def push_thumbnail(file: UploadFile = File(...), user=Depends(require_user)):
    head = await file.read(4096)
    ext = _sniff_ext(head)

    fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=ext, prefix="thumb_")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as f:
            f.write(head)
            while True:
                chunk = await file.read(1024 * 1024)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                f.write(chunk)
        return {"ok": True, "path": path}
    finally:
        await file.close()
```

这样用户传个 `xxx.weird` 也只能落地成 `.jpg/.png`。

---

## 7) 低：JWT 放 localStorage（先不动也行）

你现在 MVP 能接受，但如果你想“更稳”，路线是：
- 后端 `response.set_cookie(..., httponly=True, secure=True, samesite="lax")`
- 前端不再存 token，只走 cookie

这属于“架构层调整”，我先不强推，你要我就给一套最小 cookie 迁移版。

## 8) 补：根目录 `.gitignore`（避免 .env / db 误提交）

新建项目根目录 `.gitignore`：

```gitignore

*.db
data/

```

看你现在的描述（已部署到 VPS；文本检测用早期方案；正在把邮件、Telegram 补进来），我给你一个**务实结论**：

## 结论
- **如果你的目标是“MVP 能用”**（Google 登录 → 进入 /tools → 提交文本+封面 → 返回 `OK+code` 或 `NO`）：**基本算完整**，前提是下面的“验收清单”都过了。  
- **如果你的目标是“可长期稳定上线”**（抗滥用、可审计、可扩展到邮件/Telegram/YouTube 流程）：**还不完整**，通常还差“统一接口、审计、风控、稳定性”这几块。

---

## MVP 完整的验收清单（你现在就能自查）
### A. 登录链路
- 访问首页 → 点“切片审核工具” → 未登录会触发 Google 登录  
- 登录成功 → 自动进入 `/tools`
- `/api/me` 能返回当前用户信息（至少 email / sub / provider）

### B. 核心审核链路
- `/tools` 页面提交：`title/description/tags/subtitles + thumbnail`  
- 后端返回严格二选一：
  - `NO`  
  - `OK + XXXX-XXXX`（放行码）
- 后端**不把 token 明文展示在前端页面**（最好走 httpOnly cookie 或最少不渲染出来）

### C. 可调试（你能用 curl 复现）
- 你能在 VPS 上用 curl 直接跑通同样的审核请求
- Docker 重启后依然可用（容器自启、端口、反代都正常）

---

## “上线级完整”通常还缺什么（最常见的 8 个坑）
1. **统一一个最终入口**：最好只保留一个 `POST /api/review`，邮件/Telegram/网页表单都走同一条管线（减少分叉和漏审）。
2. **审计日志闭环**：每次审核写入 SQLite（或 Postgres）：
   - request_id、user_id、输入摘要（hash）、命中规则、结论、时间、版本号
3. **幂等与可复验**：同样输入一定得到同样的判定（尤其是 OCR/图片 hash），否则很难追责/解释。
4. **反滥用**：rate limit、IP/用户限速、上传大小上限、超限返回“上传失败”而不是 `NO`（避免误杀）。
5. **密钥管理**：Google client_secret、JWT secret、Telegram bot token（如果有）全部用环境变量，不进仓库。
6. **边界处理**：OCR 失败策略、图片解析失败策略、超时策略明确，且都写入日志。
7. **反代/缓存/文件安全**：Nginx 限制 body_size、禁止目录遍历、静态资源缓存策略别误缓存 API。
8. **健康检查与监控**：最少一个 `/healthz`，Docker/反代能探活；错误日志能定位。

---

## 我给你一个最硬核的判定方式
你把“完整”定义成一句话就行：

> **从“未登录的陌生人”到“拿到 OK/NO 结果并留痕可追溯”，全链路一次通过；重启/异常/滥用也不会把系统搞挂。**

只要你按上面两套清单跑一遍：
- **A/B/C 都过** → MVP 完整 ✅  
- **8 个坑还没补** → 生产级还不完整 ⚠️

如果你愿意更快落地：下一步优先做 **“统一 /api/review + 审计日志 + rate limit”**，这三个做完，你这个项目就从“能用”变成“能活”。

行，那就按“最小改动，最大收益”把它补成**上线级闭环**。你先做这 3 件事就够了：

## 1) 统一入口：`POST /api/review`（同时支持 JSON + multipart）
FastAPI 里用 `Request` 读 `Content-Type`，把两种输入都转成同一个内部结构，再走同一套判定管线。

```python
# app/main.py (示例骨架)
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, UploadFile, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
import hashlib, json, time

app = FastAPI()

RULESET_VERSION = "v1.0.0"

def normalize(s: str) -> str:
    return (s or "").strip().lower()

def make_input_hash(payload: dict) -> str:
    raw = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()

@app.post("/api/review")
async def review(request: Request):
    ct = (request.headers.get("content-type") or "").lower()

    # 1) 解析输入
    if "application/json" in ct:
        data = await request.json()
        title = data.get("title","")
        description = data.get("description","")
        tags = data.get("tags", []) or []
        subtitles = data.get("subtitles","")
        # 可选：data.get("thumbnail_base64") / data.get("thumbnail_url")
        thumbnail_bytes = None

    elif "multipart/form-data" in ct:
        form = await request.form()
        title = form.get("title","")
        description = form.get("description","")
        tags = form.get("tags","")  # 你也可以前端传 JSON 字符串
        subtitles = form.get("subtitles","")
        file: UploadFile | None = form.get("thumbnail")
        thumbnail_bytes = await file.read() if file else None
        # tags 规范化
        if isinstance(tags, str):
            tags = [t.strip() for t in tags.split(",") if t.strip()]

    else:
        raise HTTPException(415, "unsupported content-type")

    payload_norm = {
        "title": normalize(title),
        "description": normalize(description),
        "tags": [normalize(x) for x in (tags or [])],
        "subtitles": normalize(subtitles),
        "has_thumbnail": bool(thumbnail_bytes),
        "ruleset": RULESET_VERSION,
    }
    input_hash = make_input_hash(payload_norm)

    # 2) 调用你的检测模块（这里用伪代码占位）
    # text_hits = text_filter.check(title, description, tags, subtitles)
    # img_hits  = image_filter.check(thumbnail_bytes) if thumbnail_bytes else []
    text_hits, img_hits = [], []
    decision = "OK"
    allow_code = "ABCD-1234"
    if text_hits or img_hits:
        decision = "NO"
        allow_code = None

    # 3) 写审计日志（下一步第2点实现）
    # audit_log(...)

    if decision == "NO":
        return JSONResponse({"result": "NO", "ruleset": RULESET_VERSION, "id": input_hash[:12]})
    return JSONResponse({"result": "OK", "code": allow_code, "ruleset": RULESET_VERSION, "id": input_hash[:12]})
```

## 2) 审计日志：SQLite 一张表就行
只存**hash + 命中项 + 结论**，别存原文，风险低又可追溯。

```sql
-- sqlite: review_logs
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS review_logs (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
  user TEXT,
  ip TEXT,
  input_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  ruleset TEXT NOT NULL,
  result TEXT NOT NULL,
  allow_code TEXT,
  hits_json TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_review_logs_hash ON review_logs(input_hash);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_review_logs_ts ON review_logs(ts);
```

## 3) 反滥用交给 Nginx：限速 + 2MB 上限
最省事也最稳，直接挡住刷接口和超大图。

```nginx
# nginx.conf http {} 里
limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=review_zone:10m rate=10r/m;

server {
  client_max_body_size 2m;

  location /api/review {
    limit_req zone=review_zone burst=20 nodelay;
    proxy_pass http://backend;
  }
}
```

---

## 你用这 2 条 curl 就能验收（能跑通就是“完整”）
**JSON（给 Telegram/邮件流程用）**
```bash
curl -sS -X POST https://wjz5788.com/api/review \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"title":"test","description":"hello","tags":["a","b"],"subtitles":""}'
```

**multipart（给网页上传封面用）**
```bash
curl -sS -X POST https://wjz5788.com/api/review \
  -F "title=test" -F "description=hello" -F "tags=a,b" -F "subtitles=" \
  -F "thumbnail=@./thumb.jpg"
```

---
